# Seed 51 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small, the fastest +4.9% (Iteration 300)

`seed 51 tag2 10h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus
2,8`, commit 2ac1cc4fb (Iteration 297) - the first run with the after-seed-50 batch:
2@ and 2! as opcodes, opinline, tailpn, knumber. Started 02:54 UTC Oct 15; the
evolution 03:00-10:32 (7 h 32 min, the owner's 10 hours less the comparison's), the
front measured again by 10:54, the comparison packed by 12:50. 34,690 designs measured
(34,473 alive), 10,684 screened.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest +4.9%, smallest +0.00%, memory
+0 KB, hypervolume +1.7%, best Q +0.8% - **small**, the fastest just under its 5%.
The Q-best design of all runs is this run's f6b175f69c: 0.147 at 28,357 bytes, 168 KB
(Q 0.216 of s6's). Calibration 0.989 on cpu 2, 1.012 on cpu 8.

From the run's database (lab/evolve/seed-facts.py - every late gene now - and the
front's genomes):

- **Deaths: 217, all "reach limit at scale 0 - not run".**
- The screen: 10,684 of the 43,687 children from generation 5 skipped (24.5%).
- Children: mutation 54.0%, crossover 34.6%, borrowed 11.3% (the last fifth:
  crossover 37.8%). The final front, 162 designs: mutation 114, borrowed 21, carried
  20, crossover 7; via the attic 7.
- **The batch's genes:** opinline on 64 of the 162; 2@ expressed on 52, 2! on 1 -
  among the 69 designs without the JIT, 2@ or 2! on 8 only: **the JIT designs took
  2@**, five of the six fastest with it (the JIT translates it since Iteration 291),
  four with opinline; knumber on 8 of the 69 klookup designs; tailpn and tail engines
  on none.
- factor on 144 (level 3 on 91); clang on 21; the JIT on 93.
- In the run: fastest f7a7740238 0.129 at 28,759 (192 KB); smallest 0285b1954f
  15,647 (speed 1.480, 128 KB) - seed 50's smallest's size; least memory 10b0b5337b,
  116 KB (16,229 bytes).

---

# Evolved VM designs

34690 designs evaluated, 34473 alive, 10684 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): f6b175f69c, speed 0.141, binary + image 28,357, memory 168 KB - Q 0.205 of s6-cv8b's.

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
